### 2. [-Cities Worldwide Build and Scale AI Services With NVIDIA Physical and Agentic AI](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60773604664&p=21l&v=1&x=2NVoMLh_aflbnQp-I2K7lg)
- **Outlet(s):** M2 PressWIRE, Nvidia | **Published:** `2026-11-04` | **Sentiment:** `POSITIVE` | **Positioning Relevance Score:** `1.00`
- **Syndicated URLs:** [link](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60764804976&p=1pe&v=1&x=f8lbD1cSdgm0EtOXpoHhng)
- **Matched Innodata Pillars:** Robotic Engineering, Agent Engineering, Agent Reliability Engineering (ARE), Infrastructure Engineering
- **Matched Competitors:** Scale AI
- **Coverage Snippet:** November 4, 2026

Release date- 03112026 - From infrastructure planning and transportation to public services, cities are using NVIDIA-powered applications to address local needs and expand capacity for the broader AI ecosystem.

Cities generate enormous amounts of data from cameras, sensors and operational systems, but turning it into useful action - for planning, maintenance and public services - remains a challenge.

Physical and agentic AI offer new ways to close that gap, from digital twins for infrastructure planning to vision and service agents, while keeping local governments in control of how sensitive data is processed and used.

Building on initiatives announced at GTC Berlin, NVIDIA and its partners are expanding physical AI and AI agents to cities worldwide. Shared computing infrastructure gives city departments, neighboring municipalities and local developers a common foundation to build on - extending services without each organization having to acquire and manage resources of their own.

Agentic AI for Cities

From resident services to vision agents, cities are adopting NVIDIA agentic AI to deliver faster, more responsive public services. City teams don't need a massive infrastructure investment to get started building these AI services: depending on the workload, NVIDIA RTX workstations or a small GPU system may be enough.

In Spain, 1MillionBot combines open source ALIA models trained at the Barcelona Supercomputing Center with NVIDIA Nemotron and other models in resident-service agents for Madrid Digital (Comunidad de Madrid), Administracio Oberta de Catalunya and 150+ deployments across Spain, handling municipal procedures, appointment requests, urban-planning incidents and service inquiries.

In the San Francisco Bay Area, LiveX is bringing its AI City vision to life with NVIDIA Cosmos world models and Nemotron open models, and Omniverse and Metropolis libraries on NVIDIA RTX PRO GPUs. The interactive, conversational AI hosts understand people's needs, provide answers and take action, and can be tailored to local languages and service needs in airports, hospitals, local events and other public spaces.

Vision AI agents can turn existing city camera feeds into operational insights, helping teams identify mobility and safety issues and coordinate emergency responses across city services.

In Wroclaw, Poland, K2K is building shared AI infrastructure using NVIDIA accelerated computing, NVIDIA Cosmos world reasoning models, and NVIDIA Metropolis Blueprint for video search and summarization (VSS) to transform existing camera streams into actionable intelligence for mobility, safety and event response - using the same infrastructure across several city functions.

In Malaysia, ITMAX bundles infrastructure and agentic AI applications into long-term managed services for cities, including for traffic management. Built with reference to Cosmos, Nemotron and the Metropolis VSS blueprint, ITMAX's SamurAI V2 vision agents are scaling on NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPUs to support 17,000+ cameras by 2027. ITMAX integrates TeraSim, an open source traffic simulation platform from Laplace Intelligence, to test signal timing, reducing peak-hour corridor travel times by up to 26%.

Plan and Operate Critical Infrastructure With Digital Twins

Beyond public-facing services, digital twins can help cities expedite infrastructure planning, minimizing disruption to local communities. City digital twins can also serve as a real-time operational simulation to facilitate faster decision-making.

At Dangote's Lekki refinery in Nigeria, Honeywell Technologies' Forge Plant Cognition uses Nemotron-powered AI agents to understand operational data. A digital twin of the plant and its processes, built using Omniverse libraries, is also planned to support simulation and training. The platform, built on Dell PowerEdge servers with NVIDIA RTX PRO accelerators, helps operators identify bottlenecks, anticipate equipment and process issues, and optimize plant performance.

In Taiwan, Linker Vision and Dutch architecture firm MVRDV are using NVIDIA Omniverse and Nemotron to build environmental, social and governance agents that can assess the environmental impact of various urban planning scenarios, simulating 15-year outcomes of an urban forest canopy in 10 minutes - increasing design iteration speed from two weeks per iteration to less than an hour.

Build a Broader Local AI Ecosystem

Cities can extend AI to more services by building on infrastructure already in place and adding capacity. Shared resources and managed services can help extend those capabilities across departments and municipalities.

At Morocco's Tanger Med port, Cires Technologies is expanding a sovereign AI factory for planned applications in worker safety, congestion analysis, cargo routing and port operations. Cires Technologies is also building an ecosystem of application providers to develop local models and applications for ports and cities.

Beyond city services, Barcelona Supercomputing Center's AI Factory Accelerator Programme gives startups access to sovereign computing, technical support and training. BSC AI Factory also supports research and innovation for startups - spanning agriculture, medical imaging, knowledge access and speech services.

Learn how NVIDIA and its ecosystem are bringing physical AI, agentic AI and local AI factories to cities worldwide at Smart City Expo World Congress, running Nov. 3-5 in Barcelona, where NVIDIA Vice President of EMEA Paolo Guglielmini will deliver a keynote address followed by a panel moderated by Charbel Aoun, director of smart city and spaces for EMEA at NVIDIA.

ExlService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) is a leading global operations management and analytics company, specializing in business process outsourcing and decision analytics. The company's comprehensive service offerings include EXLS finance and accounting outsourcing, EXLS healthcare analytics, and EXLS insurance analytics, all supported by their robust analytics platform. With a focus on digital transformation, ExlService leverages cutting-edge technologies like robotic process automation and AI to enhance efficiency and customer analytics. As a strategic leader in corporate governance and compliance oversight, it drives innovation through its forward-looking initiatives, solidifying its competitive positioning in technology-driven solutions and business optimization.

Press Release article 1 of 5, Source: West Corporation, 299 words

Oct 30, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL named an OpenAI Select Partner

NEW YORK, Oct. 29, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced that it has been named an OpenAI Select Partner within the OpenAI Partner Network.

The OpenAI Partner Network is a global program that equips partners with the resources, enablement, and support to build, sell, and deliver AI solutions with OpenAI. It brings together partners with deep industry expertise, delivery capabilities, and customer relationships while equipping them with resources, enablement, and support to help enterprises adopt OpenAI frontier models and products and turn them into measurable impact.

As an OpenAI Select Partner, EXL will work with OpenAI to help organizations build, deploy, and scale AI responsibly. This work will help organizations put OpenAI frontier models to work across their business, using deeper reasoning and computer use to complete workflows and deliver finished work.

"Being named an OpenAI Select Partner reflects how we work with clients: combining frontier AI with deep understanding of their business to deliver outcomes," said Vishal Chhibbar, executive vice president and chief revenue officer, EXL. "Clients trust us to apply that capability where it matters most, across entire journeys and with accountability for the end result. That trust, built over years inside their operations is what turns AI ambition into results."

EXL brings together data, context and AI to help organizations in insurance, financial services, healthcare and life sciences, and other heavily regulated, operationally complex industries move AI from pilot to production in core enterprises. Its work includes helping insurers reimagine underwriting, claims management and administrative workflows; supporting health plans integration of AI payment integrity and patient engagement systems; and transforming risk and compliance, credit decisioning and customer engagement initiatives for financial service leaders.

Press Release article 3 of 5, Source: West Corporation, 305 words

Oct 27, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL Reports 2026 Third Quarter Results

2026 Third Quarter Revenue of $634.0 Million, up 19.7% year-over-year

Q3 Diluted EPS (GAAP) of $0.45, up 25.3% from $0.36 in Q3 of 2025

Q3 Adjusted Diluted EPS (Non-GAAP) (1) of $0.62, up 28.5% from $0.48 in Q3 of 2025

NEW YORK, Oct. 27, 2026 -- ExlService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ: EXLS), a global data and AI company, today announced its financial results for the quarter ended September 30, 2026.

Chairman and Chief Executive Officer Rohit Kapoor said, "We entered 2026 with strong momentum that has accelerated through the year, delivering third quarter revenue growth of 20% year-on-year and adjusted diluted EPS growth of 29% year-on-year. Every segment of our business grew more than 15% organically. Data and AI-led revenue, almost two-thirds of our business, grew 36% on a reported basis and 31% organically. These results reflect the strength of our data and AI strategy and our differentiated market position: deep domain expertise combined with data and AI capabilities that help clients make AI work effectively across the enterprise. Our business model affords us very good visibility into the balance of the year, and we look forward to a solid finish to 2026."

Chief Financial Officer Maurizio Nicolelli said, "Our strong third quarter performance gives us the confidence to raise our full-year outlook once again. We now expect full-year 2026 total revenue to be in the range of $2.430 billion to $2.440 billion, up from our prior guidance of $2.390 billion to $2.415 billion, reflecting 16% to 17% year-over-year growth on a reported basis and 15% on an organic constant currency basis. We now expect adjusted diluted earnings per share of $2.34 to $2.36, a 20% to 21% increase over 2025, up from our prior guidance of $2.25 to $2.29."

Press Release article 4 of 5, Source: West Corporation, 350 words

Oct 26, 2026: ExlService Holdings iMerit, an EXL Company,launches iMerit Scholars platform as AI talent war shifts to domain expertise

New platform connects domain experts with mission-critical AI work as companies compete for the human expertise needed to move AI from innovation to execution

NEW YORK, Oct. 26, 2026 -- iMerit, an EXL company, today announced the launch of its iMerit Scholars platform, designed to seamlessly manage hundreds of thousands of global domain experts (Scholars) who work onfine-tuning, evaluation and validation of generative AI models. This represents the first major launch since EXL (Nasdaq: EXLS) acquired iMerit in July to accelerate the progression of frontier AI into regulated enterprises.

The launch comes as the AI talent war heats up across generative and physical AI. The iMerit Scholars program already brings together a curated global community of 30,000 domain experts and linguists to support advanced model training, evaluation, validation and safety across specialized fields. The new iMerit Scholars platform provides a unified system to recruit, onboard, curate, assign, manage, engage, pay, and retain these experts at scale. It is powered by iMerit's proprietary Ango Hub, which allows customers and experts to collaborate on complex multimodal data to generate highly curated and validated training artifacts for high-stakes models.

"iMerit has always differentiated itself by a curated, people-first approach. We're seeing that become even more important as we scale up to help the frontier labs," said Radha Basu, executive vice president, head of iMerit at EXL. "The next phase of the GenAI race is about creating a painless and supportive infrastructure so that these highly capable specialists can enjoy their work and give their best. Scaling high quality and high engagement is mainly a human capital opportunity, backed by enabling technology. The iMerit Scholars platform is designed to create a seamless experience for top-notch talent to continue building and improving the next generation of AI."

"Now, as a part of EXL, we are expanding from the creation of models in frontier labs to the implementation of model workflows in highly regulated enterprises in banking, healthcare and insurance.

Press Release article 5 of 5, Source: West Corporation, 298 words

Oct 09, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL transforms client workflows with the integration of agentic AI into core insurance and healthcare solutions

New agentic AI capabilities compress life and annuities insurance product launch cycles, optimize in-force policy management, and move health plan payment integrity upstream

NEW YORK, Oct. 08, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced it has integrated agentic AI across core solutions for insurance and healthcare, combining workflow orchestration, enterprise data intelligence and AI-powered automation to modernize operations and deliver better experiences for its clients. As part of its portfolio of integrated AI-led solutions, EXL is embedding AI agents directly into the offerings where its clients already work. This includes reimagining two of EXL's industry-leading solutions, EXL LifePRO and EXL Payment Integrity, with AI-powered automation to help modernize operations and deliver better outcomes.

EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite addresses a central challenge confronting the life insurance industry: slow product launch cycles. Most life insurers still need eight to ten months to bring a new product to market, a constraint that limits how many products a carrier can launch each year and how quickly it can respond to regulatory or competitive changes. According to a 2025 LIMRA and Bain & Company study, approximately 100 million Americans say they need life insurance but haven't purchased it, yet just 9.4 million policies were sold in 2024. Carriers are largely competing for the same buyers, leaving a large gap in demand unaddressed.

By automating manual, repetitive workflows, EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite compresses the product launch cycle to as few as 16 weeks, leveraging AI agents to ingest product specifications, interpret rules and rates, and propose executable configurations so teams can focus on reviewing and approval rather than build from scratch.

SECTION 6 CORPORATE PROFILE

6.1 Activities

ExlService Holdings, Inc., a Nasdaq-listed company, is a global leader in data analytics, digital operations, and consulting services, primarily serving industries such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and retail. The company's core business revolves around leveraging advanced analytics, artificial intelligence, and machine learning to optimize operations, enhance decision-making, and drive efficiency for its clients. By transforming complex data into actionable insights, ExlService helps organizations streamline processes and mitigate risks. Among its fastest-growing segments are analytics-driven solutions and digital transformation services, fueled by increasing demand for AI-powered tools and cloud-based platforms. This focus has enabled the company to expand its client base and achieve strong revenue growth, positioning it as a key player in the evolving landscape of business process outsourcing and technology-enabled services. ExlService maintains a commitment to innovation and client-centric strategies, fostering sustainable growth in a competitive market. It is the NASDAQ's 6th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

Oct 09, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL transforms client workflows with the integration of agentic AI into core insurance and healthcare solutions

New agentic AI capabilities compress life and annuities insurance product launch cycles, optimize in-force policy management, and move health plan payment integrity upstream

NEW YORK, Oct. 08, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced it has integrated agentic AI across core solutions for insurance and healthcare, combining workflow orchestration, enterprise data intelligence and AI-powered automation to modernize operations and deliver better experiences for its clients. As part of its portfolio of integrated AI-led solutions, EXL is embedding AI agents directly into the offerings where its clients already work. This includes reimagining two of EXL's industry-leading solutions, EXL LifePRO and EXL Payment Integrity, with AI-powered automation to help modernize operations and deliver better outcomes.

EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite addresses a central challenge confronting the life insurance industry: slow product launch cycles. Most life insurers still need eight to ten months to bring a new product to market, a constraint that limits how many products a carrier can launch each year and how quickly it can respond to regulatory or competitive changes. According to a 2025 LIMRA and Bain & Company study, approximately 100 million Americans say they need life insurance but haven't purchased it, yet just 9.4 million policies were sold in 2024. Carriers are largely competing for the same buyers, leaving a large gap in demand unaddressed.

By automating manual, repetitive workflows, EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite compresses the product launch cycle to as few as 16 weeks, leveraging AI agents to ingest product specifications, interpret rules and rates, and propose executable configurations so teams can focus on reviewing and approval rather than build from scratch.

Aug 18, 2026: EXL closes new $1 billion senior secured credit facility

Exhibit 99.1

EXL closes new $1 billion senior secured credit facility

NEW YORK Aug. 18, 2026 EXL (NASDAQ: EXLS), a global data and AI company, announced the closing of a new credit facility with PNC Bank, N.A., as Administrative Agent, and a syndicate of lenders that allows for borrowings of up to $1 billion. Bank of America, N.A., JPMorgan Chase Bank, N.A., and TD Bank, N.A. acted as joint lead arrangers on this transaction.

The facility increases EXLs borrowing capacity from the previous limit of $600 million and provides greater covenant flexibility to support the companys business strategy. The five-year senior secured credit agreement includes a $400 million term loan, permits revolver borrowings of up to $600 million, and has an accordion feature that would allow the facility to expand equal to the greater of $470 million or 100% of EBITDA for the trailing four quarters. The term of the new agreement will expire on August 18, 2031.

This deal reflects the confidence our banking partners have in EXLs financial strength and the long-term trajectory of our business, said Maurizio Nicolelli, chief financial officer of EXL. We have consistently prioritized a strong balance sheet, and this expanded debt capacity gives us the flexibility to extend our competitive advantage through targeted mergers and acquisitions while continuing to return capital to our shareholders under our $500 million share repurchase authorization. The closing of this facility is a clear demonstration of the focused execution against our capital allocation strategy.

August 18 2026: ExlService Holdings Inc Secures $1 Billion Financing Package with PNC Bank

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has entered into a significant financing agreement with PNC Bank, National Association, that includes a $600 million revolving credit facility and a $400 million term loan facility. The agreement, finalized on 18th August 2026, positions ExlService to enhance its liquidity and financial flexibility for future initiatives.

Under the new Credit Agreement, which matures on 18th August 2031, ExlService Holdings will benefit from a comprehensive $600 million revolving credit facility, which includes a $50 million swingline loan subfacility and a $20 million letter of credit subfacility. The term loan facility will also provide ExlService with additional capital, allowing it to strategically invest in growth opportunities while maintaining a strong balance sheet.

This transition was executed without incurring early termination penalties, allowing ExlService to streamline its debt obligations.

The new financing arrangement reflects ExlService Holdings' commitment to maintaining strong financial health, supported by a robust interest coverage ratio requirement of at least 3.00 to 1.00. This project addresses the rising demand from AI and electrification and aims to create an integrated ecosystem that supports both digital growth and energy stability within the PJM Interconnection, North America's largest electric grid.

This move positions Blackstone to meet the escalating demand for AI infrastructure in the region.

Nov 2, 2026: Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX) Leads $3.1 Billion Investor Group Acquiring 49.9% Non-Controlling Stake in Mitsubishi Corporation's LNG Canada Holding Subsidiary

New York, November 2, 2026. Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX), the alternative asset management firm, on Monday announced that funds managed by Blackstone Credit & Insurance, in partnership with leading Canadian institutional investors, agreed to invest $3.1 billion to acquire a 49.9% non-controlling interest in a Mitsubishi Corporation subsidiary that holds Mitsubishi's 15% interest in the LNG Canada liquefaction business in British Columbia.

The Blackstone-led investor group includes the Canada Pension Plan Investment Board (CPP Investments), the Ontario Municipal Employees Retirement System (OMERS), the Investment Management Corporation of Ontario (IMCO) and the Public Sector Pension Investment Board (PSP Investments). LNG Canada is Canada's first and only large-scale liquefied natural gas (LNG) export terminal. The project's partners have approved a Phase 2 expansion that, once complete, will double the facility's production capacity to about 28 million tonnes per annum, making it one of the largest LNG facilities in the world. Mitsubishi Corporation, one of Japan's largest LNG suppliers, will remain the controlling partner and retain its full share of LNG offtake.

Impact on Blackstone Inc. (BX)

Following this news, Blackstone faces an expanded role in Canadian energy infrastructure, as funds managed by Blackstone Credit & Insurance lead the acquisition of a 49.9% non-controlling interest in the Mitsubishi Corporation subsidiary that holds its LNG Canada stake. The transaction positions Blackstone as a financial partner to Mitsubishi Corporation, which has more than 50 years of experience across the LNG value chain, in one of Canada's most significant energy projects, while Mitsubishi retains control and its full share of LNG offtake.

The investment builds on Blackstone's partnership with Canada's leading institutional investors and its strategy of providing large corporates with flexible solutions to unlock value from their most critical assets. Blackstone's Infrastructure and Asset Based Credit platform manages $140 billion and has over 100 investment professionals. The deal adds to a series of large-scale corporate solutions investments announced by Blackstone over the past two years in Canada and with companies building critical energy infrastructure globally, including a CDN$7 billion investment in Rogers, a CDN$2.5 billion investment in Aeroplan, a $16 billion partnership with Kuwait Oil Company and a $7 billion investment in Sempra Infrastructure Partners' Port Arthur LNG Phase 2.

ECO NEWS 2 of 5, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 335 words

Oct 27, 2026: Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX) Becomes Sole Provider of Committed Second Lien in $63 Billion Financing for Broadcom Inc. (NASDAQ:AVGO) AI Chips Leased to Anthropic PBC

New York, October 26, 2026. Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX), the world's largest alternative asset manager, on Monday announced with Bank of America, Citigroup and Morgan Stanley a $63 billion transaction to finance the purchase of Broadcom XPU AI accelerators and networking solutions for lease to Anthropic, with expected deliveries starting in calendar 2027.

The transaction will be executed through a newly formed special purpose vehicle with committed financing from the four lead financial partners. Bank of America, Citigroup and Morgan Stanley are arranging the first lien portion, while Blackstone is the sole provider of the committed portion of the second lien financing. Broadcom is providing residual value support to the vehicle for an amount equivalent to the first lien portion, as part of its previously announced strategic compute capacity agreement.

The deal adds to the hundreds of billions of dollars of debt already raised to build out AI infrastructure, with financing for chips and servers multiplying alongside data center funding.

Impact on Blackstone Inc. (BX)

Following this news, Blackstone faces a distinct position in the capital structure as the sole provider of the committed portion of the second lien financing, separate from the first lien arranged by the three banks. The role deploys the firm's credit capital directly into AI compute hardware that the special purpose vehicle will purchase from Broadcom and lease to Anthropic.

Jon Gray, President and COO of Blackstone, said the rapidly growing demand for AI is creating an enormous opportunity to finance compute capacity at scale. The transaction builds on Blackstone's existing AI infrastructure activity, as the firm was among six major financial names that partnered with Nvidia in August to mobilize more than $500 billion for AI, including helping customers fund chip purchases. Blackstone manages nearly $1.4 trillion in assets across strategies including credit and infrastructure. Kirkland & Ellis LLP is serving as legal counsel to Blackstone.

ECO NEWS 3 of 5, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 403 words

Oct 15, 2026: Galderma Group AG's $1.5 Billion All-Cash Agreement to Acquire ZO Skin Health Positions Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX) to Divest Physician-Dispensed Skincare Holding Acquired in 2020

Zug, Switzerland, October 14, 2026. Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX), the world's largest alternative asset manager, on Wednesday became party to an agreement under which Swiss dermatology company Galderma Group AG will acquire 100% of ZO Skin Health from its shareholders, including funds managed by Blackstone Tactical Opportunities and the Obagi family, for an enterprise value of $1.5 billion, with ZO Skin Health recording net sales of approximately $330 million for the twelve months ended June 2026.

The all-cash transaction, subject to customary adjustments, will be funded through a combination of existing cash reserves and bank financing. Closing is expected in the fourth quarter of 2026 or early 2027, subject to regulatory approvals and other customary closing conditions. Founded by dermatologist Dr. Zein Obagi and headquartered in Irvine, California, ZO Skin Health offers more than 40 products manufactured in the U.S. and sold through more than 16,500 physician, medical spa and online accounts across more than 40 countries.

The acquisition expands Galderma's Dermatological Skincare portfolio, which includes the Cetaphil and Alastin brands, within the fast-growing physician-dispensed skincare segment. According to industry reports, strategic buyers are increasingly prioritizing clinically backed, physician-distributed skincare brands because of their efficacy and steadier demand compared with over-the-counter products.

Impact on Blackstone (BX)

Following this news, Blackstone faces the planned divestment of its majority holding in ZO Skin Health, which funds managed by its Tactical Opportunities business acquired in 2020. The agreement values ZO Skin Health at an enterprise value of $1.5 billion in an all-cash transaction, under which Blackstone-managed funds are among the selling shareholders alongside the Obagi family.

According to Blackstone, ZO Skin Health more than doubled its revenue during the period of its partnership with Dr. Obagi and the company's management team, with the brand reaching net sales of approximately $330 million for the twelve months ended June 2026. Blackstone stated that ZO Skin Health is well positioned to continue advancing its mission under Galderma's ownership.

The transaction remains subject to regulatory approvals and other customary closing conditions, with completion expected in the fourth quarter of 2026 or early 2027. The sale concerns a single portfolio holding within Blackstone's platform of over $1.3 trillion in assets under management, which spans real estate, private equity, credit, infrastructure, life sciences, growth equity, secondaries and hedge funds.

ECO NEWS 4 of 5, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 302 words

Sep 30, 2026: Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX) Launches Norla as Dedicated Nordic Last-Mile Logistics Platform Across Sweden, Denmark and Finland

London, September 29, 2026. Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX), the alternative asset manager, on Tuesday said it is launching Norla, a Stockholm-based logistics investment platform that will give investors a more direct route into last-mile warehouses supporting e-commerce in the Nordics, with more than 200 warehouses covering over two million square metres and initial assets of about €3 billion ($3.41 billion).

The platform will include more than 200 warehouses, mainly last-mile facilities, covering over two million square metres across Sweden, Denmark and Finland. Blackstone said Norla is set to be operational in 2027 and will contain mainly assets already owned by Blackstone funds, with the possibility of adding some third-party assets. Industry reports indicate the assets will initially be worth about €3 billion ($3.41 billion).

James Seppala, head of real estate in Europe for Blackstone, said investors are increasingly seeking more granular exposure by geography and asset type. The structure is designed to provide that exposure through a dedicated Nordic last-mile logistics platform rather than through a less focused allocation.

Impact on Blackstone Inc. (BX)

Blackstone Inc. is grouping existing Nordic warehouse holdings into a dedicated last-mile logistics platform named Norla, to be based in Stockholm and operated across Sweden, Denmark and Finland. The firm said the platform will comprise more than 200 mainly last-mile warehouses covering over two million square metres and will draw mainly from assets already owned by Blackstone funds, with some third-party assets possibly included.

The assets are indicated to be worth about €3 billion ($3.41 billion) initially. Operations are targeted for 2027. The structure is intended to give investors a more direct route into Nordic last-mile logistics supporting e-commerce, aligned with stated investor demand for more granular exposure by geography and asset type.

ECO NEWS 5 of 5, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 294 words

Sep 17, 2026: Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX) Advances AI Infrastructure Buildout as Crux AI Venture Secures $22 Billion in Bank Financing

New York, September 16, 2026. Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX), an equity partner in Crux AI, on Wednesday advanced its buildout of artificial intelligence infrastructure after the venture, its cloud business formed jointly with Alphabet Inc., secured $22 billion in debt financing from a syndicate of 10 banks.

Crux AI, announced in May as a cloud business aimed at meeting growing demand for AI computing services, will use the financing to purchase Google-designed Tensor Processing Units and expand data-center capacity, with Blackstone having committed an initial $5 billion in equity to bring 500 megawatts of capacity online in 2027, with further expansion planned. The debt package is secured by the value of the purchased chips and by Crux AI's customer contracts. The bank syndicate is working to distribute a portion of the loan to additional financial institutions, and select lenders are also extending a $1 billion revolving credit line to the venture, which may later be refinanced through longer-term corporate bond issuance.

Impact on Blackstone Inc. (BX)

Following this news, Blackstone Inc. faces the ability to scale its jointly owned AI cloud infrastructure venture using external debt financing rather than relying solely on additional equity contributions. The $22 billion loan secured by Crux AI supplements Blackstone's initial $5 billion equity commitment, with the debt collateralized by the value of the purchased chips and the venture's customer contracts rather than Blackstone's own balance sheet.

This financing structure allows Blackstone to support Crux AI's plan to bring 500 megawatts of data-center capacity online in 2027, with further expansion planned, while extending the firm's exposure to AI infrastructure growth through its existing equity position without a proportional increase in direct capital outlay.

SECTION 8 CORPORATE PROFILE

8.1 Activities

Blackstone Inc., a global leader in alternative asset management and listed on the New York Stock Exchange, specializes in private equity, real estate, credit, and hedge fund investments. Founded in 1985, the firm manages a diverse portfolio of assets for institutional and individual investors, focusing on sourcing, executing, and overseeing investments that deliver long-term value across sectors like infrastructure and growth equity. Its core activities include raising capital, making strategic investments, and providing advisory services to enhance portfolio performance. Among its fastest-growing segments are private credit and real estate, fueled by rising demand for alternative financing solutions and urbanization trends, respectively. This expansion has positioned Blackstone as a key player in global markets, with a strong track record of innovation and operational excellence. It is the 3rd largest Asset manager company by market capitalization among NYSE- and TXSE-listed stocks.

Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.

Sep 09, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,’ which encapsulates the company’s commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL’s unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond’ is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we’ve built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL’s standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL’s foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL’s revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond’ is the brand that captures that. It’s an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what’s possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL’s focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond’ translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL’s transformation and the ‘Go Beyond’ brand in action, interested parties can visit the company’s official website.

Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company’s commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

Aug 18, 2026: ExlService Holdings Inc Secures $1 Billion Financing Package with PNC Bank

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has entered into a significant financing agreement with PNC Bank, National Association, that includes a $600 million revolving credit facility and a $400 million term loan facility. The agreement, finalized on 18th August 2026, positions ExlService to enhance its liquidity and financial flexibility for future initiatives.

Under the new Credit Agreement, which matures on 18th August 2031, ExlService Holdings will benefit from a comprehensive $600 million revolving credit facility, which includes a $50 million swingline loan subfacility and a $20 million letter of credit subfacility. The term loan facility will also provide ExlService with additional capital, allowing it to strategically invest in growth opportunities while maintaining a strong balance sheet.

The terms of the Credit Agreement allow ExlService Holdings to adjust borrowing rates based on its Total Net Leverage Ratio, with interest rates ranging from 1.00% to 1.75% per annum depending on the chosen benchmark. This flexibility is designed to align with the company’s financial performance, ensuring cost-effective access to capital as needed.

As part of this financial restructuring, ExlService Holdings has used proceeds from the new credit facilities to fully repay its previous obligations under an agreement with Citibank, which included a $500 million revolving credit facility and a $100 million term loan facility. This transition was executed without incurring early termination penalties, allowing ExlService to streamline its debt obligations.

The new financing arrangement reflects ExlService Holdings' commitment to maintaining strong financial health, supported by a robust interest coverage ratio requirement of at least 3.00 to 1.00. The company is also permitted to increase its borrowing capacity under certain conditions, which could further enhance its operational capabilities and strategic growth initiatives in the coming years.

SECTION 11 CORPORATE PROFILE

11.1 Activities

ExlService Holdings, Inc., a Nasdaq-listed company, is a global leader in data analytics, digital operations, and consulting services, primarily serving industries such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and retail. The company's core business revolves around leveraging advanced analytics, artificial intelligence, and machine learning to optimize operations, enhance decision-making, and drive efficiency for its clients. By transforming complex data into actionable insights, ExlService helps organizations streamline processes and mitigate risks. Among its fastest-growing segments are analytics-driven solutions and digital transformation services, fueled by increasing demand for AI-powered tools and cloud-based platforms. This focus has enabled the company to expand its client base and achieve strong revenue growth, positioning it as a key player in the evolving landscape of business process outsourcing and technology-enabled services. ExlService maintains a commitment to innovation and client-centric strategies, fostering sustainable growth in a competitive market. It is the NASDAQ's 6th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

### 9. [Journify Raises $12M Series A Amid 5x Growth](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60773535293&p=21l&v=1&x=Aju53BSkui5nxuLZ2rJn9g)
- **Outlet(s):** Computerworld, CIO | **Published:** `2026-11-04` | **Sentiment:** `POSITIVE` | **Positioning Relevance Score:** `0.87`
- **Syndicated URLs:** [link](https://www.cio.com/article/4244315/journify-raises-12m-series-a-amid-5x-growth.html)
- **Matched Innodata Pillars:** Infrastructure Engineering
- **Matched Proprietary Accelerators:** Multi-Agent E-Commerce Support
- **Matched Competitors:** Palantir
- **Coverage Snippet:** Investors back Journify's push into the US and Europe

Journify, the ad signal infrastructure company, today announced a $12 million Series A at four times its previous valuation. Al Mada Ventures led the round, co-led by returning investor Silicon Badia, with Shorooq and Bunat Ventures increasing their investment and Du Ventures and Oraseya Capital joining as new investors. The funding will take Journify into the US and Europe and expand the AI behind its platform.

Global ad spend will exceed $1 trillion in 2026, with algorithms expected to place three quarters by 2028, according to dentsu. These systems learn from the customer outcomes brands send back. Browser restrictions, broken tracking, and disconnected offline sales leave them with an incomplete picture of who buys.

Journify makes sure ad platforms such as Google, Meta, TikTok, Snap and Amazon Ads see the purchases, sign-ups and store sales a brand's customers actually make. More than 100 businesses use Journify today, from fast-growing ecommerce brands to some of the region's largest retailers, representing hundreds of millions of dollars in annual ad spend.

Customers include Jarir Bookstore, one of Saudi Arabia's largest retailers, which increased its return on ad spend on Meta by 182%, and Saudi ecommerce brand Baytonia, which grew its return on ad spend on TikTok by 80% while cutting cost per purchase by 44%.

"AI is transforming advertising, but the intelligence behind it is only as good as the signals it receives," said Taoufik El Jamali, Co-Founder and CEO of Journify. "Businesses know who buys, who comes back and what creates lasting customer value. Too little of that knowledge reaches the ad platforms driving their growth. Our vision is to close that gap, turning real customer outcomes into intelligence ad platforms can learn from. We're building toward a future where advertising AI understands what makes a customer valuable to each business, not just what makes them click.""As AI takes on a greater role in marketing, we believe the connection between reliable customer data and the decisions built on it will become increasingly valuable," said Omar Laalej, Managing Director at Al Mada Ventures. "What interests us about Journify is its approach to building that connection from the data layer up: a privacy-first foundation that can evolve into intelligence and action. The founders' understanding of that opportunity gives us confidence in their ability to build a platform that marketers can increasingly rely on, both to understand their customers and to act on that understanding."Hossam Shafick, Partner at Silicon Badia, Seed Lead and returning investor, added, "Since our initial investment, Journify has grown its valuation by more than 4x, and its revenues more than 5x, validating both the strength of its technology and the team's ability to execute at scale. That performance has deepened our conviction that Journify can become a global leader in ad signal infrastructure. We are proud to double down and co-lead this round as the company expands into the world's largest advertising markets."

Journify already uses AI to monitor every connection continuously, detect signal-quality issues and fix them. The new funding will grow those capabilities as Journify expands beyond the GCC. In the US alone, digital advertising revenue reached $294.6 billion in 2025, according to IAB and PwC.

"I'm excited to help Journify scale in the biggest ad market in the world," said Ryan Burke, a newly appointed member of Journify's board and three-time Chief Revenue Officer. "I've known Taoufik since our InVision days, and I've seen his product execution in action. Based on the success they're already seeing in the Middle East, it's not a question whether it works in the US, but how fast we can scale."

About Journify

Journify builds ad signal infrastructure for performance marketers. Its privacy-first, real-time data layer helps brands improve the quality and reliability of the first-party signals used in advertising and measurement.

Journify connects data across web, mobile, offline sales, CRM, ecommerce platforms, backend systems and data warehouses. It validates and enriches conversion events and activates consented signals server-side across Google, Meta, TikTok, Snap, Amazon Ads and other platforms.

Founded in 2022 by Taoufik El Jamali, Amine Chouki and Omar Al Shoubaki, Journify is headquartered in San Francisco with offices in Dubai and Riyadh. Learn more at journify.io.

Journify has obtained a SOC 2 Type II report and is committed to GDPR compliance.

### 24. [Appoints Korea Managing Director, Confirms Plans to Enter South Korean Market](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60767544903&p=21l&v=1&x=Rmd5366ZByouCvB2dFqTlg)
- **Outlet(s):** Market News Publishing | **Published:** `2026-11-03` | **Sentiment:** `POSITIVE` | **Positioning Relevance Score:** `0.72`
- **Matched Innodata Pillars:** Robotic Engineering
- **Matched Competitors:** Mercor, Turing, Surge AI, Invisible Technologies
- **Coverage Snippet:** XPENG INC ("XPEV-N") - Appoints Korea Managing Director, Confirms Plans to Enter South - Korean Market //st * XPENG today announced the appointment of Lee Man-sik, a 30-year automotive industry veteran with a proven track record in sales and commercial operations, as Managing Director of XPENG Motors Korea, effective December 1, 2026. * The appointment marks XPENG's planned entry into the South Korean market. Details on launch timing and local business operations will be announced at a later date.

XPeng Inc. ("XPENG" or "the Company), a leading global Physical AI company, today announced the appointment of Lee Man-sik as Managing Director of XPENG Motors Korea, as the company prepares to bring its smart vehicles to one of Asia's most sophisticated automotive markets.

Mr. Lee brings more than three decades of automotive industry experience. Having begun his career in engineering, he has built expertise across sales, marketing, product strategy, brand communications, dealer network development and customer service. He joined Volvo Cars Korea in 2003 and played a pivotal role in establishing Volvo as a leading premium import brand in South Korea.

At Volvo Cars Korea, he led the development and execution of the brand's mid- to long-term growth strategy, with responsibilities spanning brand positioning, product planning, sales and marketing, nationwide dealer expansion and customer experience.

"Over the past thirty years, I have learned that sustainable growth for a premium brand rests not only on outstanding products but on trust built with customers," said Mr. Lee. "XPENG is a brand presenting new possibilities for the industry through innovative AI technology and a vision for future mobility. I am delighted to join this journey of change."

On the Korean market, he added: "Korean customers hold exceptionally high expectations for automobiles and are remarkably open to new technology and innovation. We believe XPENG's advanced technology and product competitiveness can offer them new choices and differentiated value. Above all, our goal is to build a brand customers can choose with confidence one that delivers a trustworthy experience at every step of ownership."

In his new role, Mr. Lee will lead XPENG's efforts to establish its business foundation in South Korea, drawing on his deep understanding of the local automotive market and extensive experience in premium brand operations. His remit will include developing sales and service networks, building strategic partnerships and strengthening XPENG's brand presence in the market.

XPENG's planned entry into South Korea comes as the company continues to deepen its international footprint this year. In October 2026, the company delivered 50,237 vehicles worldwide up 22% from September and 20% from a year earlier, a new monthly record. Overseas deliveries climbed to 13,326 units, surging 166% year on year to set a record of their own. The company now operates across 83 countries and regions, supported by eight R&D centers, six manufacturing bases and more than 1,200 stores worldwide. At the 2026 Paris Motor Show in October, XPENG launched its next-gen AI flagship SUV, the G9L, for the European market and showcased its latest intelligent driving technologies, while expanding opportunities for global technology partnerships. XPENG aims to bring that same differentiated technology and customer experience to South Korea, and details on launch timing and local operations will be shared in due course.

About XPENG

XPENG is a leading global Physical AI company, dedicated to bringing artificial intelligence into the physical world to reshape future mobility and smart living. Through in-house R&D, XPENG has developed a full-stack Physical AI architecture spanning Turing AI chips, world foundation models, and highly integrated software and hardware applications. This unified technology foundation of XPENG powers an expansive product portfolio of smart EVs, robotaxis, and humanoid robots, advancing the deployment of Physical AI at scale. Headquartered in Guangzhou, China, XPENG is dual-primary listed on the New York Stock Exchange and the Hong Kong Stock Exchange. With global capabilities across R&D, manufacturing, sales, and services, XPENG drives continuous technological innovation and fosters an open Physical AI ecosystem, making life smarter, safer, and better for users worldwide. For more information, please visit https://www.xpeng.com/.

- **Coverage Snippet:** Asian stock markets are trading mostly lower on Tuesday, following the mixed cues from Wall Street overnight, as South Korean markets tumbled amid weakness in semiconductor stocks on concerns over returns on huge AI spending and mixed earnings and cautious forecasts from major US technology firms. Asian markets closed mixed on Monday.

Meanwhile, reports suggesting a possible de-escalation in the Middle East sent crude oil price tumbling, lowering inflation concerns and capping market losses.

Iran's news agency quoted Iran's President Masoud Pezeshkian as stating that Iran's Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamanei granted powers to the government in its talks with the U.S. in the ongoing efforts to end the war.

Over the weekend, Secretary of State Marco Rubio said President Donald Trump would like to resolve the U.S.-Iran war "diplomatically."

Qatar submitted revised proposals to convince Iran for an agreement with the U.S. Iran acknowledged that discussions are ongoing.

Despite the uncertainty on the outcome and the hard stance shown by leaders of both the nations, traders welcomed these developments although with cautious optimism.

The Australian stock market is modestly higher on Tuesday, reversing the losses in the previous session, following the mixed cues from Wall Street overnight. The benchmark S&P/ASX 200 index is moving well above the 8,850 level, with gains across most sectors led by mining and technology stocks. Energy stocks were the only weak spot on tumbling crude oil prices.

Traders remain cautious ahead of the Reserve Bank's interest rate decision later in the day, with the markets widely expecting a fifth rate hike by 25 basis points to 4.85 percent, a 18-year high.

The benchmark S&P/ASX 200 Index is gaining 22.40 points or 0.25 percent to 8,878.90, after touching a high of 8,909.10 earlier. The broader All Ordinaries Index is up 3.10 points or 0.04 percent to 8,980.30. Australian stocks closed notably lower on Monday.

Among the major miners, BHP Group and Fortescue are gaining almost 1 percent each, while Mineral Resources is advancing almost 2 percent and Rio Tinto is edging up 0.1 percent.

Oil stocks are mostly lower. Beach energy is losing almost 3 percent, Santos is declining almost 4 percent, Woodside Energy is slipping almost 5 percent and Origin Energy is edging down 0.2 percent.

Among tech stocks, Afterpay owner Block is gaining almost 1 percent, Xero is adding more than 2 percent, Zip is up more than 3 percent and WiseTech Global is advancing almost 3 percent, while Appen is declining more than 4 percent.

Gold miners are mostly higher. Evolution Mining, Genesis Minerals, Resolute Mining and Northern Star Resources are all gaining almost 3 percent each, while Newmont is adding more than 2 percent.

Among the big four banks, ANZ Banking and Westpac are losing almost 1 percent each, while National Australia Bank is edging down 0.2 percent. Commonwealth Bank is gaining almost 1 percent.

In the currency market, the Aussie dollar is trading at $0.700 on Tuesday.

The Japanese stock market is closed for Culture Day on Tuesday. Japanese shares ended modestly lower on Monday.

In the currency market, the U.S. dollar is trading in the higher 157 yen-range on Tuesday.

Elsewhere in Asia, South Korea is tumbling 2.6 percent, while New Zealand, China, Singapore, Taiwan and Indonesia are lower by between 0.3 and 1.0 percent each. Hong Kong and Malaysia are down 0.5 and 0.2 percent, respectively.

On Wall Street, stocks showed a strong move to the upside in early trading on Monday but saw a significant downturn over the course of the session. The major averages pulled back well off their early highs, with the Nasdaq and S&P 500 sliding into negative territory.

The tech-heavy Nasdaq slumped 272.68 points or 1.1 percent to 25,164.32 and the S&P 500 fell 38.67 points or 0.5 percent to 7,541.40, while the narrower Dow ended the day up 213.67 points or 0.4 percent at 51,983.98.

Meanwhile, the major European markets all moved to the upside on the day. While the French CAC 40 Index shot up by 1 percent, the German DAX Index climbed by 0.6 percent and the U.K.'s FTSE 100 Index rose by 0.3 percent.

Crude oil prices tumbled on Monday on reports that discussions are ongoing to settle the conflict between the U.S. and Iran. West Texas Intermediate crude for December delivery was down $7.76 or 7.45 percent at $96.36 per barrel.

Meanwhile, the major European markets ended the day mixed. While the U.K.'s FTSE 100 Index crept up by 0.1 percent, the French CAC 40 Index closed just below the unchanged line and the German DAX Index slid by 0.9 percent.

Crude oil prices spiked on Friday on increased supply disruption concerns, with media reports suggesting renewed U.S. military action against Iran this week. West Texas Intermediate crude Oil for December delivery was up $2.39 or 2.34 percent at $104.39 per barrel.

Wed Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,' which encapsulates the company's commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we've built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL's standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL's revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond' is the brand that captures that. It's an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what's possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL's focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond' translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL's transformation and the ‘Go Beyond' brand in action, interested parties can visit the company's official website.

Thu Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

Wed Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,' which encapsulates the company's commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we've built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL's standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL's revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond' is the brand that captures that. It's an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what's possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL's focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond' translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL's transformation and the ‘Go Beyond' brand in action, interested parties can visit the company's official website.

Thu Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

SECTION 2 CORPORATE PROFILE

2.1 Activities

ExlService Holdings, Inc., a Nasdaq-listed company, is a global leader in data analytics, digital operations, and consulting services, primarily serving industries such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and retail. The company's core business revolves around leveraging advanced analytics, artificial intelligence, and machine learning to optimize operations, enhance decision-making, and drive efficiency for its clients. By transforming complex data into actionable insights, ExlService helps organizations streamline processes and mitigate risks. Among its fastest-growing segments are analytics-driven solutions and digital transformation services, fueled by increasing demand for AI-powered tools and cloud-based platforms. This focus has enabled the company to expand its client base and achieve strong revenue growth, positioning it as a key player in the evolving landscape of business process outsourcing and technology-enabled services. ExlService maintains a commitment to innovation and client-centric strategies, fostering sustainable growth in a competitive market. It is the NASDAQ's 6th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

- **Coverage Snippet:** TELUS CORP ("T-T;TU-N") - New TELUS collective agreement ratified by the United - Steelworkers membership

TELUS today announced that team members represented by the Telecommunications Workers Union, United Steelworkers Local 1944 (USW) bargaining unit have ratified the new agreement reached by the parties earlier this month. This new agreement, which has a term of April 1, 2027 to December 31, 2031, will apply to more than 3,000 TELUS team members nationally.

"Thank you to our team members for supporting this agreement and to both bargaining teams for their constructive work," said Victor Dodig, President and CEO of TELUS. "Reaching an agreement well ahead of the current contract's expiry provides stability for our team members and our business, allowing us to focus together on delivering the reliable service and outstanding experiences our customers count on."

About TELUS

TELUS (TSX: T, NYSE: TU) is a leading Canadian communications technology company operating in more than 45 countries and generating over $20 billion in annual revenue with more than 17 million customer connections through our advanced suite of broadband services for consumers, businesses and the public sector. TELUS is passionate about putting our customers and communities first, leading the way globally in client service excellence and social capitalism. The TELUS PureFibre and 5G networks connect Canadians at home, at work and in the communities where they live.

TELUS Health is enhancing approximately 159 million lives across 200 countries and territories through innovative preventive medicine and well-being technologies. TELUS Agriculture &Consumer Goods utilizes digital technologies and data insights to optimize the connection between producers and consumers. TELUS Digital specializes in digital customer experiences and future-focused digital transformations that deliver value for their global clients.

Since 2000, TELUS and our team members have contributed more than $1.85 billion in cash, in-kind contributions, time and programs to communities across Canada and around the world.

For more information, visit telus.com.
